Generated by `aideleg.paper.headlines.render_log` from `docs/redteam/challenges.csv`. Do not edit by hand.

# Red-team log: every headline number

AEI data are conversations matched to O*NET tasks. Nothing here says anything about job risk, displacement or reassurance about the labour market.

## Challenges

- **C1** Reproduce. A clean rebuild (Task 33's fresh clone, or `make` in a clean worktree) gives the same printed value.
- **C2** Provenance. The value traces to one generated file, and its spec, sample and inference match the PAP section cited (quote the §).
- **C3** Timing. Every input that is claimed to be pre-formation is pre-formation. Look-ahead inputs are labelled.
- **C4** Protocol. For P-H5 and P-RQ1: the attempt marker, the single-run commit, no later edit to its files (`test_confirmatory_files_untouched`).
- **C5** Specification search. The value is the pre-registered primary or carries its robustness/exploratory label. No "best of" selection.
- **C6** Units. pp vs decimal, per SD, ×252, percent 0–100, years vs days.
- **C7** Sample accounting. n, dropped rows/industries and dropped shares are reported and reconcile with the source table.
- **C8** Influence. Softw/top-leverage units; the robustness rows that move the value most are named. This is reported, never changed.
- **C9** Testing family. The family, the Holm level and the α split are stated correctly. Variants are not counted.
- **C10** Wording. AEI usage wording; P14 wording for non-rejections; no causal claim; equal prominence for nulls.
- **C11** Uncertainty. CI and p are present. The MDE sits beside each market null.
- **C12** Alternative explanations. Classifier and platform drift (H2), suppression and the R7 bounds (H1/H3), task mix (H4), tech beta (R7 row) and collinearity (H5b).

Referee recommendation: wording fixed: state that the measured rise is a single r2-to-r3 step coinciding with the change of classified-conversation base (filtered vs not_classified+none), so drift cannot be separated from behaviour. Resolved: requirement W-3 (docs/redteam/wording_requirements.md). | fails | wording fixed | 0ffc66d |

## `rq1.H2.decision`

PAP §2.0 RQ2 row: "`z_usage`, `z_delegation`, `z_augmentation` vs AIOE, Eloundou \| FF49, Design B and A_pooled \| Spearman ρ \| industry bootstrap CI"; CI = 2.5/97.5 percentile of 10,000 industry resamples (src/aideleg/describe/rq2.py spearman_bootstrap, n_draws_used=10000), n=49. Registry pap_section cites '§2.1 D8', but D8 governs the Eloundou rating only; the AIOE benchmark's construction is governed by D6 (O*NET-detail union crosswalk, PAP §2.1 D6), which the registry does not cite. Referee recommendation: label fixed: registry pap_section for the z_aioe keys should cite §2.1 D6 (AIOE vintage crosswalk) in place of/alongside D8. But OEWS May 2024 weights (exposure.py _inputs load_national(2024); build_exposure load_industry_matrix(2024,4)) were released 2025-04-02 (PAP §10 S1, USDL-25-0451) and O*NET 29.3 task statements in May 2025 (S1; C-74: byte-identical to 29.2, so only the OEWS weights differ in practice). PAP §2.1 says Design B is 'not strictly real-time'. could_vs_does.md labels Design B only 'Design B (primary)' with no timing caveat (A_pooled is labelled 'exploratory; contains look-ahead (PAP §10, S3)'; R1 is labelled 'robustness; look-ahead' in robustness/summary.md). R32 (strict real-time, OEWS May 2023) applies to H5/H5b only per PAP §8.2, so no RQ2 strict-real-time value exists. Referee recommendation: label fixed: RQ2 tables/prose state that Design B's OEWS May 2024 weights (released 2025-04-02) and O*NET 29.3 links postdate 2025-03-27 (PAP §10 S1); B is out of sample in AEI data only. The registered keys are not the largest: B z_delegation/z_eloundou (0.716) is the smallest B cell and z_augmentation (0.783-0.850) and gpt4 cells (0.815-0.850) are higher and not registered, so no best-of selection. Primary construction: unmatched='zero' (D3), equal split (D4), split crosswalk (D23), hq=exclude (D7). | holds | none needed |  |
| C6 Units | unit of the value traced to the generating code and the PAP section; arithmetic recomputed where the inputs are printed | value '0.7629591837'; Spearman ρ and its bounds are unitless, in [-1,1]; CI is the 2.5/97.5 percentile industry bootstrap (describe/rq2.py:46,59; results/rq2.py:31-32), 10000 draws used, n=49 (could_vs_does.csv row); '95%' in the registry quantity is correct | holds | none needed |  |
| C7 Sample accounting | reconciled n, bootstrap draws and dropped employment against the audit tables | could_vs_does.csv:5 n=49, n_draws_used=10000 (no draw dropped for constant vectors). All 49 FF49 industries present (docs/audit/tables/exposure_coverage.md B row: industries 49, covered 0.680-0.988, median 0.890). Excluded employment reconciles in docs/audit/tables/crosswalk_coverage.md (split, hq=exclude): NAICS 5511 2,809,200 (1.84%), government 10,685,940 (7.00%), unmapped private 393,171 (0.26% of total, 0.28% of private). Zero-assigned 232/831 occupations, 12.14% (could_vs_does.md D3 section = oews_coverage.md B row). Benchmark FF49 coverage AIOE 0.976-1.000, Eloundou 0.949-0.998 (benchmark_coverage.md). | holds | none needed |  |
| C8 Influence | leave-one-industry-out Spearman on Design B (49 runs) and read every RQ2 robustness row (R1, R2, R3, R11, R14, R15, R17, R20, R33; R6 and R32 have no RQ2 part per PAP §8.2) | Point rho 0.762. Leave-one-out: largest rise dropping Txtls +0.035, largest fall dropping Meals -0.014; Softw (z_delegation 4.739 SD, top leverage for H5) moves rho by -0.011 only (rank statistic). Rows moving rho most (docs/results/robustness/summary.md, R*.md): R3 unmatched=drop 0.643 [0.420, 0.801] (largest move, D3); R2 wage-bill weights 0.817; R33 emp-weighted crosswalk 0.746; R20 majority crosswalk 0.760 (46 industries, C-71); R1 OEWS 2025 (look-ahead) 0.768; R11 0.762; R14 BLS AIOE crosswalk 0.766; R15 and R17 identical by construction (C-70; labelling only). R6 (FF30/48) and R32 (strict real-time) are H5/H5b-only. Reported, not changed. | holds | none needed |  |
| C9 Testing family | family, Holm level and α split checked against the committed p's and PAP §3.3/§3.4/§4.0/D22; Holm recomputed with fractions.Fraction | value '0.7629591837'; RQ2 is descriptive: PAP §2.0 :252 family 'none', decision rule 'descriptive'; no test, no α | n/a | none needed |  |
| C10 Wording | read could_vs_does.md, rq2/README.md and results/rq2.py for usage wording, causal language and prominence | could_vs_does.md line 3 carries the WORDING ('AEI data are conversations matched to O*NET tasks. Nothing here says anything about job risk, displacement or reassurance about the labour market.'); Scope section: 'RQ2 is descriptive. Nothing in this document is a hypothesis test or a decision'; no causal claim; Design A_pooled table printed with the same columns as B; P14 wording not applicable (no test, PAP §2.0 RQ2 'descriptive', family 'none'). Figure axis label 'does; conversations matched to O*NET tasks' (results/rq2.py fig_could_vs_does). The zero-assignment phrasing is challenged under d3.zero_assigned_pct C10. | holds | none needed |  |
| C11 Uncertainty | checked that an interval is attached and whether p/MDE apply | could_vs_does.csv:5 spearman 0.7629591837, ci_low 0.5940684155, ci_high 0.8656925801, 10000 draws; could_vs_does.md Scope states the intervals are 2.5/97.5 industry-bootstrap percentile intervals that describe spread. No p by design (PAP §2.0 RQ2: inference 'industry bootstrap CI', family 'none', decision 'descriptive'); MDE applies to market nulls only, so not required here. | holds | none needed |  |
| C12 Alternative explanations | checked alternative explanations in scope: crosswalk/coverage, benchmark construction, zero-assignment; considered the shared-weight mechanism | Zero-assignment: R3 (unmatched='drop') lowers rho to 0.643 [0.420, 0.801], the largest move of any row: the 232 zero-assigned occupations (12.14%; mostly manual trades, oews_coverage.md) are also low on the benchmarks, so part of the agreement comes from the D3 assumption; FF49 zero-assigned share reaches 0.47 in some industries (computed via exposure.aggregate_to_ff49 on has_usage; FF49-weighted 11.93% vs national 12.14%). Crosswalk: R20 0.760, R33 0.746; 5511: R15 identical (C-70). Benchmark construction: R14 0.766; AIOE union crosswalk raises coverage 82.5%->98.9% (benchmark_coverage.md); Eloundou coverage 0.949-0.998. Not addressed anywhere: both sides are aggregated with the same OEWS occupation weights w_oj (exposure.aggregate_to_ff49; C-65), so the cross-industry rho partly reflects shared occupational composition (any occupation score correlated with white-collar share would correlate); no row or note separates this. Referee recommendation: wording fixed: RQ2 prose states that rho inherits the common occupation-mix weights on both sides and that rho depends on the D3 zero assumption (R3 lowers it most). Resolved: requirement W-7 (docs/redteam/wording_requirements.md). | fails | wording fixed | 0ffc66d |

| C1 Reproduce | resolve() the key; map source_file to the controller-ruled regenerations (Plan 2 results regen, Plan 3 Task 14 rebuild, Plan 4 Task 15 make robustness / C-80, confirmatory single-run commits) | resolved value '0.5940684155' from docs/results/rq2/could_vs_does.csv (csv:design=B,aei=z_delegation,benchmark=z_aioe:ci_low); tests/results/test_generated.py:36-41,86-95 regenerates docs/results/rq2 (rq2.report) and compares every .md/.csv byte for byte; rerun now: `uv run pytest tests/results/test_generated.py::test_results_docs_up_to_date` -> 1 passed (14.99 s), tree clean; could_vs_does.csv has one commit (0fd71cb); re-verified at Task 33 | holds | none needed |  |
| C2 Provenance | resolve() + recompute from derived parquet; compare spec/sample/inference with the PAP sections the registry cites | docs/results/rq2/could_vs_does.csv:5 (design=B,z_delegation,z_aioe) ci_low=0.5940684155; resolve() returns 0.5940684155; generated by aideleg.results.rq2 (src/aideleg/results/rq2.py could_vs_does); recomputed point rho from data/derived/exposure_ff49.parquet x benchmark_ff49.parquet (scipy spearmanr) = 0.7629591837 exactly. PAP §2.0 RQ2 row: "`z_usage`, `z_delegation`, `z_augmentation` vs AIOE, Eloundou \| FF49, Design B and A_pooled \| Spearman ρ \| industry bootstrap CI"; CI = 2.5/97.5 percentile of 10,000 industry resamples (src/aideleg/describe/rq2.py spearman_bootstrap, n_draws_used=10000), n=49. Registry pap_section cites '§2.1 D8', but D8 governs the Eloundou rating only; the AIOE benchmark's construction is governed by D6 (O*NET-detail union crosswalk, PAP §2.1 D6), which the registry does not cite. Referee recommendation: label fixed: registry pap_section for the z_aioe keys should cite §2.1 D6 (AIOE vintage crosswalk) in place of/alongside D8. Resolved: PAP §11 C-81 (2): headlines.csv pap_section now cites §2.1 D6. | fails | label fixed | 94b6a62 |
| C3 Timing | checked Design B input vintages against the 2025-03-27 formation date (PAP §2.1 timing para, §10 S1, §8.2 R32, §11 C-74) and the labels in could_vs_does.md | AEI input is r2 only (exposure.py design_occupations: B reads r2 claude_ai), published 2025-03-27; AIOE and Eloundou predate it. But OEWS May 2024 weights (exposure.py _inputs load_national(2024); build_exposure load_industry_matrix(2024,4)) were released 2025-04-02 (PAP §10 S1, USDL-25-0451) and O*NET 29.3 task statements in May 2025 (S1; C-74: byte-identical to 29.2, so only the OEWS weights differ in practice). PAP §2.1 says Design B is 'not strictly real-time'. could_vs_does.md labels Design B only 'Design B (primary)' with no timing caveat (A_pooled is labelled 'exploratory; contains look-ahead (PAP §10, S3)'; R1 is labelled 'robustness; look-ahead' in robustness/summary.md). R32 (strict real-time, OEWS May 2023) applies to H5/H5b only per PAP §8.2, so no RQ2 strict-real-time value exists. Referee recommendation: label fixed: RQ2 tables/prose state that Design B's OEWS May 2024 weights (released 2025-04-02) and O*NET 29.3 links postdate 2025-03-27 (PAP §10 S1); B is out of sample in AEI data only. ## `rq2.B.z_delegation.z_eloundou.rho`

| challenge | check | evidence | outcome | resolution | commit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 Reproduce | resolve() the key; map source_file to the controller-ruled regenerations (Plan 2 results regen, Plan 3 Task 14 rebuild, Plan 4 Task 15 make robustness / C-80, confirmatory single-run commits) | resolved value '0.7158163265' from docs/results/rq2/could_vs_does.csv (csv:design=B,aei=z_delegation,benchmark=z_eloundou:spearman); tests/results/test_generated.py:36-41,86-95 regenerates docs/results/rq2 (rq2.report) and compares every .md/.csv byte for byte; rerun now: `uv run pytest tests/results/test_generated.py::test_results_docs_up_to_date` -> 1 passed (14.99 s), tree clean; could_vs_does.csv has one commit (0fd71cb); re-verified at Task 33 | holds | none needed |  |
| C2 Provenance | resolve() + recompute from derived parquet; compare spec/sample/inference with the PAP sections the registry cites | docs/results/rq2/could_vs_does.csv:6 (design=B,z_delegation,z_eloundou) spearman=0.7158163265; resolve() returns 0.7158163265; generated by aideleg.results.rq2 (src/aideleg/results/rq2.py could_vs_does); recomputed point rho from data/derived/exposure_ff49.parquet x benchmark_ff49.parquet (scipy spearmanr) = 0.7158163265 exactly. PAP §2.0 RQ2 row: "`z_usage`, `z_delegation`, `z_augmentation` vs AIOE, Eloundou \| FF49, Design B and A_pooled \| Spearman ρ \| industry bootstrap CI"; CI = 2.5/97.5 percentile of 10,000 industry resamples (src/aideleg/describe/rq2.py spearman_bootstrap, n_draws_used=10000), n=49. D8 (human β = headline z_eloundou) matches the benchmark column used. | holds | none needed |  |
| C3 Timing | checked Design B input vintages against the 2025-03-27 formation date (PAP §2.1 timing para, §10 S1, §8.2 R32, §11 C-74) and the labels in could_vs_does.md | AEI input is r2 only (exposure.py design_occupations: B reads r2 claude_ai), published 2025-03-27; AIOE and Eloundou predate it. But OEWS May 2024 weights (exposure.py _inputs load_national(2024); build_exposure load_industry_matrix(2024,4)) were released 2025-04-02 (PAP §10 S1, USDL-25-0451) and O*NET 29.3 task statements in May 2025 (S1; C-74: byte-identical to 29.2, so only the OEWS weights differ in practice). PAP §2.1 says Design B is 'not strictly real-time'. could_vs_does.md labels Design B only 'Design B (primary)' with no timing caveat (A_pooled is labelled 'exploratory; contains look-ahead (PAP §10, S3)'; R1 is labelled 'robustness; look-ahead' in robustness/summary.md). R32 (strict real-time, OEWS May 2023) applies to H5/H5b only per PAP §8.2, so no RQ2 strict-real-time value exists. Referee recommendation: label fixed: RQ2 tables/prose state that Design B's OEWS May 2024 weights (released 2025-04-02) and O*NET 29.3 links postdate 2025-03-27 (PAP §10 S1); B is out of sample in AEI data only. Resolved: requirement W-9 (docs/redteam/wording_requirements.md). | fails | wording fixed | 0ffc66d |
| C4 Protocol | checked whether P-H5/P-RQ1 apply; git log on the source file | RQ2 is descriptive (PAP §2.0 table :252, 'descriptive'), under neither P-H5 nor P-RQ1; own generation: git log -- docs/results/rq2/could_vs_does.csv -> one commit 0fd71cb (2026-10-22), regenerates byte-identically (test_results_docs_up_to_date passed now) | n/a | none needed |  |
| C5 Specification search | checked that the value is the pre-registered primary design/benchmark and not a best-of pick among the 18 RQ2 cells | PAP §2.1: Design B = r2 only; A_r2 and A_pooled exploratory; D8 makes z_eloundou the headline with z_eloundou_gpt4 beside it; §2.0 lists AIOE and Eloundou. could_vs_does.md prints all 9 B cells and all 9 A_pooled cells. The registered keys are not the largest: B z_delegation/z_eloundou (0.716) is the smallest B cell and z_augmentation (0.783-0.850) and gpt4 cells (0.815-0.850) are higher and not registered, so no best-of selection. Primary construction: unmatched='zero' (D3), equal split (D4), split crosswalk (D23), hq=exclude (D7). | holds | none needed |  |
| C6 Units | unit of the value traced to the generating code and the PAP section; arithmetic recomputed where the inputs are printed | value '0.7158163265'; Spearman ρ and its bounds are unitless, in [-1,1]; CI is the 2.5/97.5 percentile industry bootstrap (describe/rq2.py:46,59; results/rq2.py:31-32), 10000 draws used, n=49 (could_vs_does.csv row); '95%' in the registry quantity is correct | holds | none needed |  |
| C7 Sample accounting | reconciled n, bootstrap draws and dropped employment against the audit tables | could_vs_does.csv:6 n=49, n_draws_used=10000 (no draw dropped for constant vectors). All 49 FF49 industries present (docs/audit/tables/exposure_coverage.md B row: industries 49, covered 0.680-0.988, median 0.890). Excluded employment reconciles in docs/audit/tables/crosswalk_coverage.md (split, hq=exclude): NAICS 5511 2,809,200 (1.84%), government 10,685,940 (7.00%), unmapped private 393,171 (0.26% of total, 0.28% of private). Zero-assigned 232/831 occupations, 12.14% (could_vs_does.md D3 section = oews_coverage.md B row). Benchmark FF49 coverage AIOE 0.976-1.000, Eloundou 0.949-0.998 (benchmark_coverage.md). | holds | none needed |  |
| C8 Influence | leave-one-industry-out Spearman on Design B (49 runs) and read every RQ2 robustness row (R1, R2, R3, R11, R14, R15, R17, R20, R33; R6 and R32 have no RQ2 part per PAP §8.2) | Point rho 0.715. Leave-one-out: largest rise dropping Rtail +0.032, largest fall dropping Meals -0.017; Softw (z_delegation 4.739 SD, top leverage for H5) moves rho by -0.015 only (rank statistic). Rows moving rho most (docs/results/robustness/summary.md, R*.md): R3 unmatched=drop 0.568 [0.321, 0.754] (largest move, D3); R2 wage-bill weights 0.786; R33 emp-weighted crosswalk 0.708; R20 majority crosswalk 0.714 (46 industries, C-71); R1 OEWS 2025 (look-ahead) 0.724; R11 0.721; R14 BLS AIOE crosswalk 0.716; R15 and R17 identical by construction (C-70; labelling only). R6 (FF30/48) and R32 (strict real-time) are H5/H5b-only. Reported, not changed. | holds | none needed |  |
| C9 Testing family | family, Holm level and α split checked against the committed p's and PAP §3.3/§3.4/§4.0/D22; Holm recomputed with fractions.Fraction | value '0.7158163265'; RQ2 is descriptive: PAP §2.0 :252 family 'none', decision rule 'descriptive'; no test, no α | n/a | none needed |  |
| C10 Wording | read could_vs_does.md, rq2/README.md and results/rq2.py for usage wording, causal language and prominence | could_vs_does.md line 3 carries the WORDING ('AEI data are conversations matched to O*NET tasks. Nothing here says anything about job risk, displacement or reassurance about the labour market.'); Scope section: 'RQ2 is descriptive. Nothing in this document is a hypothesis test or a decision'; no causal claim; Design A_pooled table printed with the same columns as B; P14 wording not applicable (no test, PAP §2.0 RQ2 'descriptive', family 'none'). Figure axis label 'does; conversations matched to O*NET tasks' (results/rq2.py fig_could_vs_does). The zero-assignment phrasing is challenged under d3.zero_assigned_pct C10. | holds | none needed |  |
| C11 Uncertainty | checked that an interval is attached and whether p/MDE apply | could_vs_does.csv:6 spearman 0.7158163265, ci_low 0.5265726916, ci_high 0.8392496258, 10000 draws; could_vs_does.md Scope states the intervals are 2.5/97.5 industry-bootstrap percentile intervals that describe spread. No p by design (PAP §2.0 RQ2: inference 'industry bootstrap CI', family 'none', decision 'descriptive'); MDE applies to market nulls only, so not required here. | holds | none needed |  |
| C12 Alternative explanations | checked alternative explanations in scope: crosswalk/coverage, benchmark construction, zero-assignment; considered the shared-weight mechanism | Zero-assignment: R3 (unmatched='drop') lowers rho to 0.568 [0.321, 0.754], the largest move of any row: the 232 zero-assigned occupations (12.14%; mostly manual trades, oews_coverage.md) are also low on the benchmarks, so part of the agreement comes from the D3 assumption; FF49 zero-assigned share reaches 0.47 in some industries (computed via exposure.aggregate_to_ff49 on has_usage; FF49-weighted 11.93% vs national 12.14%). Crosswalk: R20 0.714, R33 0.708; 5511: R15 identical (C-70). Benchmark construction: R14 0.716; AIOE union crosswalk raises coverage 82.5%->98.9% (benchmark_coverage.md); Eloundou coverage 0.949-0.998. Not addressed anywhere: both sides are aggregated with the same OEWS occupation weights w_oj (exposure.aggregate_to_ff49; C-65), so the cross-industry rho partly reflects shared occupational composition (any occupation score correlated with white-collar share would correlate); no row or note separates this. Referee recommendation: wording fixed: RQ2 prose states that rho inherits the common occupation-mix weights on both sides and that rho depends on the D3 zero assumption (R3 lowers it most). Resolved: requirement W-7 (docs/redteam/wording_requirements.md). | fails | wording fixed | 0ffc66d |

| C9 Testing family | family, Holm level and α split checked against the committed p's and PAP §3.3/§3.4/§4.0/D22; Holm recomputed with fractions.Fraction | value '0.8392496258'; RQ2 is descriptive: PAP §2.0 :252 family 'none', decision rule 'descriptive'; no test, no α | n/a | none needed |  |
| C10 Wording | read could_vs_does.md, rq2/README.md and results/rq2.py for usage wording, causal language and prominence | could_vs_does.md line 3 carries the WORDING ('AEI data are conversations matched to O*NET tasks. Nothing here says anything about job risk, displacement or reassurance about the labour market.'); Scope section: 'RQ2 is descriptive. Nothing in this document is a hypothesis test or a decision'; no causal claim; Design A_pooled table printed with the same columns as B; P14 wording not applicable (no test, PAP §2.0 RQ2 'descriptive', family 'none'). Figure axis label 'does; conversations matched to O*NET tasks' (results/rq2.py fig_could_vs_does). The zero-assignment phrasing is challenged under d3.zero_assigned_pct C10. | holds | none needed |  |
| C11 Uncertainty | checked that an interval is attached and whether p/MDE apply | could_vs_does.csv:6 spearman 0.7158163265, ci_low 0.5265726916, ci_high 0.8392496258, 10000 draws; could_vs_does.md Scope states the intervals are 2.5/97.5 industry-bootstrap percentile intervals that describe spread. No p by design (PAP §2.0 RQ2: inference 'industry bootstrap CI', family 'none', decision 'descriptive'); MDE applies to market nulls only, so not required here. | holds | none needed |  |
| C12 Alternative explanations | checked alternative explanations in scope: crosswalk/coverage, benchmark construction, zero-assignment; considered the shared-weight mechanism | Zero-assignment: R3 (unmatched='drop') lowers rho to 0.568 [0.321, 0.754], the largest move of any row: the 232 zero-assigned occupations (12.14%; mostly manual trades, oews_coverage.md) are also low on the benchmarks, so part of the agreement comes from the D3 assumption; FF49 zero-assigned share reaches 0.47 in some industries (computed via exposure.aggregate_to_ff49 on has_usage; FF49-weighted 11.93% vs national 12.14%). Crosswalk: R20 0.714, R33 0.708; 5511: R15 identical (C-70). Benchmark construction: R14 0.716; AIOE union crosswalk raises coverage 82.5%->98.9% (benchmark_coverage.md); Eloundou coverage 0.949-0.998. Not addressed anywhere: both sides are aggregated with the same OEWS occupation weights w_oj (exposure.aggregate_to_ff49; C-65), so the cross-industry rho partly reflects shared occupational composition (any occupation score correlated with white-collar share would correlate); no row or note separates this. Referee recommendation: wording fixed: RQ2 prose states that rho inherits the common occupation-mix weights on both sides and that rho depends on the D3 zero assumption (R3 lowers it most). Resolved: requirement W-7 (docs/redteam/wording_requirements.md). | fails | wording fixed | 0ffc66d |

| C2 Provenance | resolve() + recompute from derived parquet; compare spec/sample/inference with the PAP sections the registry cites | docs/results/rq2/could_vs_does.csv:2 (design=B,z_usage,z_aioe) spearman=0.8093877551; resolve() returns 0.8093877551; generated by aideleg.results.rq2 (src/aideleg/results/rq2.py could_vs_does); recomputed point rho from data/derived/exposure_ff49.parquet x benchmark_ff49.parquet (scipy spearmanr) = 0.8093877551 exactly. PAP §2.0 RQ2 row: "`z_usage`, `z_delegation`, `z_augmentation` vs AIOE, Eloundou \| FF49, Design B and A_pooled \| Spearman ρ \| industry bootstrap CI"; CI = 2.5/97.5 percentile of 10,000 industry resamples (src/aideleg/describe/rq2.py spearman_bootstrap, n_draws_used=10000), n=49. Registry pap_section cites '§2.1 D8', but D8 governs the Eloundou rating only; the AIOE benchmark's construction is governed by D6 (O*NET-detail union crosswalk, PAP §2.1 D6), which the registry does not cite. Referee recommendation: label fixed: registry pap_section for the z_aioe keys should cite §2.1 D6 (AIOE vintage crosswalk) in place of/alongside D8. Resolved: PAP §11 C-81 (2): headlines.csv pap_section now cites §2.1 D6. | fails | label fixed | 94b6a62 |
| C3 Timing | checked Design B input vintages against the 2025-03-27 formation date (PAP §2.1 timing para, §10 S1, §8.2 R32, §11 C-74) and the labels in could_vs_does.md | AEI input is r2 only (exposure.py design_occupations: B reads r2 claude_ai), published 2025-03-27; AIOE and Eloundou predate it. But OEWS May 2024 weights (exposure.py _inputs load_national(2024); build_exposure load_industry_matrix(2024,4)) were released 2025-04-02 (PAP §10 S1, USDL-25-0451) and O*NET 29.3 task statements in May 2025 (S1; C-74: byte-identical to 29.2, so only the OEWS weights differ in practice). PAP §2.1 says Design B is 'not strictly real-time'. could_vs_does.md labels Design B only 'Design B (primary)' with no timing caveat (A_pooled is labelled 'exploratory; contains look-ahead (PAP §10, S3)'; R1 is labelled 'robustness; look-ahead' in robustness/summary.md). R32 (strict real-time, OEWS May 2023) applies to H5/H5b only per PAP §8.2, so no RQ2 strict-real-time value exists. Referee recommendation: label fixed: RQ2 tables/prose state that Design B's OEWS May 2024 weights (released 2025-04-02) and O*NET 29.3 links postdate 2025-03-27 (PAP §10 S1); B is out of sample in AEI data only. Resolved: requirement W-9 (docs/redteam/wording_requirements.md). | fails | wording fixed | 0ffc66d |

PAP §2.1 says Design B is 'not strictly real-time'. could_vs_does.md labels Design B only 'Design B (primary)' with no timing caveat (A_pooled is labelled 'exploratory; contains look-ahead (PAP §10, S3)'; R1 is labelled 'robustness; look-ahead' in robustness/summary.md). R32 (strict real-time, OEWS May 2023) applies to H5/H5b only per PAP §8.2, so no RQ2 strict-real-time value exists. Referee recommendation: label fixed: RQ2 tables/prose state that Design B's OEWS May 2024 weights (released 2025-04-02) and O*NET 29.3 links postdate 2025-03-27 (PAP §10 S1); B is out of sample in AEI data only. Resolved: requirement W-9 (docs/redteam/wording_requirements.md). | fails | wording fixed | 0ffc66d |
| C4 Protocol | checked whether P-H5/P-RQ1 apply; git log on the source file | RQ2 is descriptive (PAP §2.0 table :252, 'descriptive'), under neither P-H5 nor P-RQ1; own generation: git log -- docs/results/rq2/could_vs_does.csv -> one commit 0fd71cb (2026-10-22), regenerates byte-identically (test_results_docs_up_to_date passed now) | n/a | none needed |  |
| C5 Specification search | checked that the value is the pre-registered primary design/benchmark and not a best-of pick among the 18 RQ2 cells | PAP §2.1: Design B = r2 only; A_r2 and A_pooled exploratory; D8 makes z_eloundou the headline with z_eloundou_gpt4 beside it; §2.0 lists AIOE and Eloundou. could_vs_does.md prints all 9 B cells and all 9 A_pooled cells. The registered keys are not the largest: B z_delegation/z_eloundou (0.716) is the smallest B cell and z_augmentation (0.783-0.850) and gpt4 cells (0.815-0.850) are higher and not registered, so no best-of selection. Primary construction: unmatched='zero' (D3), equal split (D4), split crosswalk (D23), hq=exclude (D7). | holds | none needed |  |
| C6 Units | unit of the value traced to the generating code and the PAP section; arithmetic recomputed where the inputs are printed | value '0.8953718277'; Spearman ρ and its bounds are unitless, in [-1,1]; CI is the 2.5/97.5 percentile industry bootstrap (describe/rq2.py:46,59; results/rq2.py:31-32), 10000 draws used, n=49 (could_vs_does.csv row); '95%' in the registry quantity is correct | holds | none needed |  |
| C7 Sample accounting | reconciled n, bootstrap draws and dropped employment against the audit tables | could_vs_does.csv:2 n=49, n_draws_used=10000 (no draw dropped for constant vectors). All 49 FF49 industries present (docs/audit/tables/exposure_coverage.md B row: industries 49, covered 0.680-0.988, median 0.890). Excluded employment reconciles in docs/audit/tables/crosswalk_coverage.md (split, hq=exclude): NAICS 5511 2,809,200 (1.84%), government 10,685,940 (7.00%), unmapped private 393,171 (0.26% of total, 0.28% of private). Zero-assigned 232/831 occupations, 12.14% (could_vs_does.md D3 section = oews_coverage.md B row). Benchmark FF49 coverage AIOE 0.976-1.000, Eloundou 0.949-0.998 (benchmark_coverage.md). | holds | none needed |  |
| C8 Influence | leave-one-industry-out Spearman on Design B (49 runs) and read every RQ2 robustness row (R1, R2, R3, R11, R14, R15, R17, R20, R33; R6 and R32 have no RQ2 part per PAP §8.2) | Point rho 0.809. Leave-one-out: largest rise dropping Txtls +0.031, largest fall dropping Meals -0.011; Softw (z_delegation 4.739 SD, top leverage for H5) moves rho by -0.008 only (rank statistic). Rows moving rho most (docs/results/robustness/summary.md, R*.md): R3 unmatched=drop 0.727 [0.532, 0.851] (largest move, D3); R2 wage-bill weights 0.847; R33 emp-weighted crosswalk 0.786; R20 majority crosswalk 0.797 (46 industries, C-71); R1 OEWS 2025 (look-ahead) 0.813; R11 0.810; R14 BLS AIOE crosswalk 0.811; R15 and R17 identical by construction (C-70; labelling only). R6 (FF30/48) and R32 (strict real-time) are H5/H5b-only. Reported, not changed. | holds | none needed |  |
| C9 Testing family | family, Holm level and α split checked against the committed p's and PAP §3.3/§3.4/§4.0/D22; Holm recomputed with fractions.Fraction | value '0.8953718277'; RQ2 is descriptive: PAP §2.0 :252 family 'none', decision rule 'descriptive'; no test, no α | n/a | none needed |  |
| C10 Wording | read could_vs_does.md, rq2/README.md and results/rq2.py for usage wording, causal language and prominence | could_vs_does.md line 3 carries the WORDING ('AEI data are conversations matched to O*NET tasks. Nothing here says anything about job risk, displacement or reassurance about the labour market.'); Scope section: 'RQ2 is descriptive. Nothing in this document is a hypothesis test or a decision'; no causal claim; Design A_pooled table printed with the same columns as B; P14 wording not applicable (no test, PAP §2.0 RQ2 'descriptive', family 'none'). Figure axis label 'does; conversations matched to O*NET tasks' (results/rq2.py fig_could_vs_does). The zero-assignment phrasing is challenged under d3.zero_assigned_pct C10. | holds | none needed |  |
| C11 Uncertainty | checked that an interval is attached and whether p/MDE apply | could_vs_does.csv:2 spearman 0.8093877551, ci_low 0.6612997391, ci_high 0.8953718277, 10000 draws; could_vs_does.md Scope states the intervals are 2.5/97.5 industry-bootstrap percentile intervals that describe spread. No p by design (PAP §2.0 RQ2: inference 'industry bootstrap CI', family 'none', decision 'descriptive'); MDE applies to market nulls only, so not required here. | holds | none needed |  |
| C12 Alternative explanations | checked alternative explanations in scope: crosswalk/coverage, benchmark construction, zero-assignment; considered the shared-weight mechanism | Zero-assignment: R3 (unmatched='drop') lowers rho to 0.727 [0.532, 0.851], the largest move of any row: the 232 zero-assigned occupations (12.14%; mostly manual trades, oews_coverage.md) are also low on the benchmarks, so part of the agreement comes from the D3 assumption; FF49 zero-assigned share reaches 0.47 in some industries (computed via exposure.aggregate_to_ff49 on has_usage; FF49-weighted 11.93% vs national 12.14%). Crosswalk: R20 0.797, R33 0.786; 5511: R15 identical (C-70). Benchmark construction: R14 0.811; AIOE union crosswalk raises coverage 82.5%->98.9% (benchmark_coverage.md); Eloundou coverage 0.949-0.998. Not addressed anywhere: both sides are aggregated with the same OEWS occupation weights w_oj (exposure.aggregate_to_ff49; C-65), so the cross-industry rho partly reflects shared occupational composition (any occupation score correlated with white-collar share would correlate); no row or note separates this. Referee recommendation: wording fixed: RQ2 prose states that rho inherits the common occupation-mix weights on both sides and that rho depends on the D3 zero assumption (R3 lowers it most). Resolved: requirement W-7 (docs/redteam/wording_requirements.md). | fails | wording fixed | 0ffc66d |

Not addressed anywhere: both sides are aggregated with the same OEWS occupation weights w_oj (exposure.aggregate_to_ff49; C-65), so the cross-industry rho partly reflects shared occupational composition (any occupation score correlated with white-collar share would correlate); no row or note separates this. Referee recommendation: wording fixed: RQ2 prose states that rho inherits the common occupation-mix weights on both sides and that rho depends on the D3 zero assumption (R3 lowers it most). Resolved: requirement W-7 (docs/redteam/wording_requirements.md). | fails | wording fixed | 0ffc66d |
